===== a) list_tags =====
$ curl -sS -i -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7373/mcp -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -H 'Accept: application/json, text/event-stream' -H 'Authorization: Bearer <TEST_TOKEN>' --data '{"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 6, "method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": "list_tags", "arguments": {}}}'

date: Tue, 27 Oct 2026 13:33:40 GMT
